  // global variables
  const notIE  = !(window.navigator.userAgent.indexOf("MSIE ") > 0 || !!navigator.userAgent.match(/Trident.*rv\:11\./))

  // get cookie function read the value of specific cookie
  function getCookie(cname) {
      var name = cname + "=";
      var decodedCookie = decodeURIComponent(document.cookie);
      var ca = decodedCookie.split(';');
      for (var i = 0; i < ca.length; i++) {
          var c = ca[i];
          while (c.charAt(0) == ' ') {
              c = c.substring(1);
          }
          if (c.indexOf(name) == 0) {
              return c.substring(name.length, c.length);
          }
      }
      return null;
  }

  // function to hide subscription pencil
  function hidearcpencil() {
      var styleNode = document.createElement("style");
      var styleText = document.createTextNode("#subscription-pencil-area { display:none  !important; }");
      styleNode.setAttribute("id", "hidepencil");
      styleNode.type = "text/css";
      styleNode.appendChild(styleText);
      document.getElementsByTagName("head")[0].appendChild(styleNode);
  }

  // hide subscription pencil on pagebuilder mode
  if(window.location.href.indexOf('.tgam.arcpublishing.com') > -1){
    hidearcpencil();
  }

    /**
   * Detect if the browser is running in Private Browsing mode
   *
   * @export
   * @returns {Promise}
   */
  function isPrivateMode() {
    return new Promise(function (resolve) {
      const on = function (){ return resolve(true);}// is in private mode
      const off = function(){ return resolve(false);} // not private mode
      const testLocalStorage = function () {
        try {
          if (localStorage.length) off();
          else {
            localStorage.x = 1;
            localStorage.removeItem('x');
            off();
          }
        } catch (e) {
          // Safari only enables cookie in private mode
          // if cookie is disabled then all client side storage is disabled
          // if all client side storage is disabled, then there is no point
          // in using private mode
          navigator.cookieEnabled ? on() : off();
        }
      };
      // Chrome & Opera
      if (window.webkitRequestFileSystem) {
        return void window.webkitRequestFileSystem(0, 0, off, on);
      }
      // Firefox
      if ('MozAppearance' in document.documentElement.style) {
        const db = indexedDB.open('test');
        db.onerror = on;
        db.onsuccess = off;
        return void 0;
      }
      // Safari
      const isSafari = navigator.userAgent.match(/Version\/([0-9\._]+).*Safari/);
      if (isSafari) {
        const version = parseInt(isSafari[1], 10);
        if (version >= 11) {
          try {
            var dbname = "hasDB";
            window.openDatabase(dbname, "1.0", dbname, 0);
            return off();
          } catch (_) {
            return on();
          };
        } else if (version < 11) {
          return testLocalStorage();
        }
      }
      // if (/constructor/i.test(window.HTMLElement)) {
      //   return testLocalStorage();
      // }

      // IE10+ & Edge
      if (!window.indexedDB && (window.PointerEvent || window.MSPointerEvent)) {
        return on();
      }
      // others
      return off();
    });
  }

  // set local storage cookie if incognito
  if(notIE){
    isPrivateMode().then(function (isPrivate) {
      localStorage.setItem('isPrivate', isPrivate);
    });
  }

  // AdBlock check
  function isAdBlocked(){
      var adElement = document.getElementsByClassName('c-ad')[0];
      var isadblock = adElement ? !( adElement.offsetWidth || adElement.offsetHeight || adElement.getClientRects().length ) : true;
      return isadblock;
  }

  // hide the pencil or the element
  function hideUnit(testid, pencilid) {
   setDarwinState(testid, 'hasClosed', true);
   document.getElementById(pencilid).className = 'u-hidden';
   event.target.setAttribute("aria-expanded", false);
  }

  // right now doesnt do anything much
  // but this can be useful if we want to define properties that cannot be changed
  // or seal the object in order to not delete keys.
  function DarwinState(key, value) {
      this.Key = key;
      this.Value = value;
  }

  // get province or city according to datalayer postal code
  function getprovince_city(){
    var pCodeFirstChar="";
    if(window.tgam.datalayer && window.tgam.datalayer.identity && window.tgam.datalayer.identity.postal){
      pCode =  window.tgam.datalayer.identity.postal;
      pCodeFirstChar = pCode.substr(0, 1).toUpperCase();
      pCodeFirstThreeChar =  pCode.substr(0, 3).toUpperCase();
      if(pCodeFirstChar == 'V'){
        return 'BC';
      }else if(pCodeFirstChar == 'T'){
        return 'AB';
      }else if (pCodeFirstChar == 'L' || pCodeFirstChar == 'M'){
        return 'ON';
      }else if (pCodeFirstChar == 'K'){
        return 'OT';
      }else if (pCodeFirstChar == 'B'){
        return 'HF';
      }else if (pCodeFirstChar == 'G' || pCodeFirstChar == 'H'){
        return 'QC';
      }else if(pCodeFirstThreeChar == 'N5V'){
        return 'L';
      }else{
        return '';
      }
    }else{
      return pCodeFirstChar;
    }
  }

  // count the number of views for homepage, for custom units
  if(window.location.href === 'https://www.theglobeandmail.com/'){
    var count = getCookie('homepgfeature') ? Number(getCookie('homepgfeature'))+1:1;
    count = count > 15 ? 0 : count;
    document.cookie = 'homepgfeature='+ count;
  }

  // schedule the test
  function schedule(start, end){
        var today= new Date();
        return (new Date(start) <= today) && (today <= new Date(end));
  }

  // set number of views
  function setFrequency(pencilid){
    console.log(getCookie(pencilid));
    if (getCookie(pencilid) !== "true") {
      var count = getCookie(pencilid)==null? 1: parseInt(getCookie(pencilid))+1;
      document.cookie = pencilid + "=" + count + ";path=/";
    }
  }

  // call apis
 function getapi(url, compare){
    if (window.navigator.userAgent.indexOf("MSIE ") === -1) {
     return fetch(url)
     .then(function (resp) {
         return resp.json();
     })
     .then(function (data) {
       if (url.indexOf('userprofilescore') > -1 ) {
          // cem-1369
          if (typeof(data) === 'number'){
            pushTracking1369(data);
          } else {
            pushTracking1369(-1);
          }
       }
      })
     .catch(function (error) {
        return error;
     });
    } else {
      return false;
    }
  }

    // call graphiql apis
 function getapi_graphiql(url, compare){
    if (window.navigator.userAgent.indexOf("MSIE ") === -1) {
     return fetch(url,{
        method: "POST",
        body: compare[0]
    })
     .then(function (resp) {
         return resp.json();
     })
     .then(function (data) {
       var output = [];
       output[0] = compare[1];
       output[1] = data;
       document.cookie = "getloginsessions_api= " + JSON.stringify(output) + ";" + "expires="+ new Date(new Date().getTime()+60*60*1000*24).toGMTString()+";path=/";
        return  getArticle(data);
     })
     .catch(function (error) {
         return error;
     });
    } else {
      return false;
    }
  }

            monthcount = ++monthcount; // calculate total months
            //console.log(data[monthNo].weeklyFeatures);
            for (var property1 in data[monthNo].weeklyFeatures) {

                  // console.log(data[monthNo].weeklyFeatures[property1].deviceFeatures.Computer.articleSectionCount)

                  weekcount = ++weekcount; // calculate desktop total weeks

                }
              }

              } else {
                articlesOther = articlesOther + o[property2];
              }
            }
          })

          var otherReader = (articlesOther / totalArticlesRead)*100;
          var articlesPerWeek = (totalArticlesRead / weekcount);

  function _typeof(obj) {
    if (typeof Symbol === "function" && typeof Symbol.iterator === "symbol") {
      _typeof = function _typeof(obj) {
        return typeof obj;
      };
    } else {
      _typeof = function _typeof(obj) {
        return obj && typeof Symbol === "function" && obj.constructor === Symbol && obj !== Symbol.prototype ?
          "symbol" : typeof obj;
      };
    }
    return _typeof(obj);
  }

    id: 'v-7',

    text: 'not contain sequential characters like \'abc\', \'123\' \'098\' or \'321\'',
    validate: function validate(value) {

      var testValue = value.toLowerCase();
      var circularNumbers = [{
        value: '0',
        next: 1,
        prev: 9
      }, {
        value: '1',
        next: 2,
        prev: 0

      var _loop = function _loop(_i) {
        if (/([0-9])/g.test(testValue[+_i].toString())) {

            if (item.value === testValue[+_i]) {
              var firstNext = circularNumbers[item.next];
              var firstPrev = circularNumbers[item.prev];
              var secondNext = circularNumbers[firstNext.next];
              var secondPrev = circularNumbers[firstPrev.prev];

              if (testValue[+_i + 1] === firstNext.value && testValue[+_i + 2] === secondNext.value ||
                testValue[+_i + 1] === firstPrev.value && testValue[+_i + 2] === secondPrev.value) {
                isSequential = true;
              }
            }
          });
          if (isSequential) return {
            v: false
          };
        } else if (String.fromCharCode(testValue.charCodeAt(_i) + 1) === testValue[+_i + 1] && String
          .fromCharCode(testValue.charCodeAt(_i) + 2) === testValue[+_i + 2]) {
          return {
            v: false
          };
        }
      };

      for (var _i in value) {
        var _ret = _loop(_i);

        if (_typeof(_ret) === "object") return _ret.v;
      }

      return true;
    }
  }, {
    id: 'v-8',
    isValid: true,
    text: 'not contain \'qwerty\' sequences',
    validate: function validate(value) {
      if (!value) return false;
      var qwerty = 'qwertyuiopasdfghjklzxcvbnm~!@#$%^&*()_+';
      var password = value.toLowerCase();

      for (var i in value) {

      return true;

  // error message functions
  function hideError(errorField) {
    errorField.setAttribute("hidden", "hidden");
  }

  function hideAllErrors() {
    email_err.setAttribute("hidden", "hidden");
    pass_err.setAttribute("hidden", "hidden");
    form_err.setAttribute("hidden", "hidden");
  }

  function showError(errorField, message) {
    errorField.textContent = message;
    errorField.removeAttribute("hidden");
  }

  function keyUpEmail() {
    if (document.getElementById('form-error').innerText.indexOf('confirmation') > -1) {
      hideError(form_err);
    }
  }


  // email address selection & verify
  function getAddress() {
    address = document.querySelector("#modal-dialog-nl-email-cem1369").value.trim();
  }

  var isValidEmailAddress = function isValidEmailAddress(addr) {
    var re =
      /^(([^<>()[\]\\.,;:\s@\"]+(\.[^<>()[\]\\.,;:\s@\"]+)*)|(\".+\"))@((\[[0-9]{1,3}\.[0-9]{1,3}\.[0-9]{1,3}\.[0-9]{1,3}\])|(([a-zA-Z\-0-9]+\.)+[a-zA-Z]{2,}))$/;
    var email = addr.trim();
    return re.test(email);
  }

  // password validation on keyup and submit click
  function validatePassword() {
    hideError(pass_err);

    /* unhide password rules & checklist */
    var passwordRuleText = document.querySelector('.password-check');
    if (passwordRuleText.hasAttribute('hidden')) {
      passwordRuleText.removeAttribute('hidden');
    }

    var passwordValue = document.querySelector('.js-modal-dialog-nl-password').value;
    validRules = []; // storing rule id's here, correspond with li elements

    passwordRules.forEach(function (rule) {
      if (rule.validate(passwordValue)) {
        rule.isValid = true;
        validRules.push(rule.id); // validRules.length == 7; means all have passed

        document.getElementById(rule.id).classList.add('valid'); // change li to green if valid
      } else {
        rule.isValid = false;
        document.getElementById(rule.id).classList.remove('valid');
      }
    }); // END passwordRules.forEach

    // this hides password field when typed password fulfills all rules
    if (validRules.length != 7) {
      passwordRuleText.removeAttribute('hidden');
    } else {
      passwordRuleText.setAttribute('hidden', 'hidden');
    }

  }

  function checkPassBeforeSubmit() {
    if (validRules.length != 7) {
      showError(pass_err, "Weak password, please check the password rules and try again.");
      return false;
    }

    return true;
  };

  function validateRegistrationAccount(check_val) {
    getAddress()
    // will return data on if email can be accepted, or if user already has an account
    var endpoint = "https://gamservices" + (!window.tgam.env.isProd ? ".stg2" : "") +
      ".theglobeandmail.com/usermanagement/api/v3/user/validate";

    fetch(endpoint, {
        method: "POST",
        credentials: "include",
        headers: {
          "Content-Type": "application/json;charset=UTF-8",
          Accept: "application/json"
        },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          email: address
        })
      })
      .then(function (response) {
        return response.json()
      })
      .then(function (responseData) {
        console.log(responseData);

        if (responseData.result === true) {
          console.log('success');
          hideError(email_err);

          if (checkPassBeforeSubmit() && check_val === 'signup') {
            hideAllErrors();
            readyToRegister = true;
            // place email value into hidden form
            document.getElementById('modal-dialog-nl-email').value = address;
            // submit old form
            document.getElementsByClassName('js-modal-dialog-nl-submit')[0].click();
            // WAIT FOR CAPTCHA TO PASS OR FAIL
            observeCaptcha();

          }

        } else if (responseData.data.email.errors.ALREADY_TAKEN) {
          console.warn(responseData.data.email.errors.ALREADY_TAKEN);
          showError(email_err, "This email is already in use. Please login or try another email addresss.");
        } else if (responseData.data.email.errors.NOT_EMAIL) {
          console.warn('please enter a correct email');
          showError(email_err, "Please enter a correct email address.");
        } else {
          console.warn('problem, email could not be validated at this time');
          showError(email_err,
            "There was a probem with validating this email with our records, please try another email address.");
        }

      })
      .catch(function (err) {
        console.warn("[ CEM-1369 ] fetch error");
        showError(email_err, "There was a probem with connection, please try again later.");
      })
  }

  function registerUser(email) {
    var passwordValue = document.querySelector('.js-modal-dialog-nl-password').value;
    var params = {
      countryCode: "CA",
      email: email,
      firstName: "Globe",
      lastName: "Reader",
      password: passwordValue
    };
    var endpoint = "https://gamservices" + (!window.tgam.env.isProd ? ".stg2" : "") +
      ".theglobeandmail.com/usermanagement/api/v3/user";


    fetch(endpoint, {
        method: "POST",
        credentials: "include",
        headers: {
          "Content-Type": "application/json;charset=UTF-8",
          Accept: "application/json"
        },
        body: JSON.stringify(params)
      })
      .then(function (response) {
        return response.json()
      })
      .then(function (responseData) {
        trackConversion();

        // hide form
        document.getElementById("cem1369_newsletter_signup").className = "success";
        document.querySelector('.email-var').innerText = email;
        setDarwinState('cem1369', 'hasCreatedAccount', true);
        hasCreatedAccount = true;
        document.cookie =
          "afterLoginGoTo=https://www.theglobeandmail.com/newsletters/?cid=cem1369-activated;domain=.theglobeandmail.com;path=/";

      })
      .catch(function (err) {
        console.warn("[ CEM-1369 ] fetch error");
        showError(email_err, "There was a probem with connection, please try again later.");
      })

  };

  // upon submit click, do all checks
  function signupButtonClick() {
    event.preventDefault();
    event.stopPropagation();
    hideAllErrors();
    //console.log("CEM-1369 | signupButton | stopped event default and propagation");

    getAddress();
    if (isValidEmailAddress(address)) {
      //console.log("CEM-1369 | signupButtonClick() | isValidEmailAddress() | TRUE"); // check password strength
      validateRegistrationAccount('signup');
    }
  }

  var captchaPassed = function captchaPassed() {
    // check if this is the right pass or not
    console.log('captcha has passed');
    var email_ssu = document.getElementById('modal-dialog-nl-email').value;
    var email_reg = document.getElementById('modal-dialog-nl-email-cem1369').value;

    if (email_ssu !== "" && email_ssu !== email_reg) {
      document.querySelector('.email-var').innerText = email_ssu;
    }

    // if user has previously created an account, show confirm email messaging
    if (hasCreatedAccount && document.querySelector('.js-modal-dialog-nl-contents').classList.contains('ready-reg')) {
      document.getElementsByClassName('js-modal-dialog-nl-contents')[0].classList.replace('ready-reg', 'reg-signup');
    }

    if (readyToRegister) {
      document.getElementsByClassName('js-modal-dialog-nl-contents')[0].classList.add('ready-reg');
      registerUser(address);
      readyToRegister = false; // reset captcha success
    }


  }

  function captchaFailed() {
    signup_button.disabled = true;
    signup_button.innerHTML = 'ROBOT CHECK FAILED!';
    showError(form_err, "Your device was verified as a robot, please refresh the page and try again");
  }

  function observeCaptcha() {

    window.tgam.api.loadRecaptcha()
      .then(function () {
        console.log("[ CEM-1369 ] observeCaptcha() | captcha loaded ");

        window.tgam.api.executeCaptcha("newsletter_cem1369")
          .then(function (response) {
            if (response === "invalid") {
              console.log("[ CEM-1369 ] observeCaptcha() | captcha invalid ");
              captchaFailed();
            } else if (response === "valid") {
              console.log("[ CEM-1369 ] observeCaptcha() | captcha valid ");
              captchaPassed();
            }
          })

      })
  }

        "variantName": "experience",
        "segmentName": "anon-sect"
      }
    });

    console.log('CEM-1369 | trackconversion() | tracking pushed');
  }

  function trackConversion() {
    if (getCookie('sp_tgamid.a05e') && getCookie('sp_tgamid.a05e').split('.')[0]) {
      getapi("https://tgamuserprofile.theglobeandmail.ca/main/userprofilescore?sophiId=" + getCookie('sp_tgamid.a05e')
        .split('.')[0], 'ssu');
    } else {
      pushTracking1369(-1)
    }
  }

  function trackLogin() {
    if (getSegment("device", "desktop")) {
      // close newsletter modal prior to opening login modal
      document.getElementsByClassName('c-button c-button--white-primary c-button--icon-only c-button--no-border')[0]
        .click();
      window.tgam.ui.login.show();
      document.getElementById("login-modal").getElementsByTagName('iframe')[0].src =
        "https://sec.theglobeandmail.com/user/#/modal/login?cid=cem1369-login";
    } else {
      window.location.href = "https://sec.theglobeandmail.com/user/#/login?cid=cem1369-login";
    }
  }

  /* CEM-1369 new functions */
  // DOM elements


  function attachFirstSignUpListener() {
    try {
      // executes each time for a particular mutation type and on observed element
      var mutationObserver = new MutationObserver((mutations) => {
        const targetClass = "js-modal-dialog-nl-address";
        var m = mutations;
        for (let i = 0; i < m.length; i++) {
          var mRec = m[i] // mutation record, short mRec
          var condition1 =
            mRec.target.className === targetClass;

          if (condition1) {
            console.warn(
              "[ CEM-1369 ] newsletter subscribed, value stored for second newsletter check in localstorage ");
            var storeEmail = document.querySelector('.js-modal-dialog-nl-address').innerText.trim()
            setDarwinState('cem1369', 'hasSignedUpOnce', storeEmail);
            mutationObserver.disconnect()
            break;
          }
        }
      }); // END mutationObserver object

      var captchaDOMcheck = null;
      /* check every second if element has been created,
          when found attach mutation listener and stop checking */
      var doesElementExist = setInterval(function () {
        captchaDOMcheck = document.querySelector('.js-modal-dialog-nl-contents');

        if (captchaDOMcheck != null) {
          //console.log("CEM-1369 | observeCaptcha() | DOM target: ".concat(captchaDOMcheck)); // start listening, disconnect() to when garbage

          mutationObserver.observe(captchaDOMcheck, {
            // attributes: true,
            // characterData: true,
            childList: true,
            subtree: true
            // attributeOldValue: true,
            // characterDataOldValue: true
          });
          //console.log('CEM-1369 | observeCaptcha() | mutation listener attached');
          clearInterval(doesElementExist);
        }
      }, 1000);
    } catch (error) {
      console.warn("CEM-1369 | attachFirstSignUpListener() | Error: ".concat(error));
    }
  }

  function signupNewsletterModal() {
    /*
     * @description What to show when clicking sign-up button
     * @example
     *   setDarwinState('cem1369', 'hasSignedUpOnce', 'test@test.com');
     */
    var hasSignedUp = getDarwinState().cem1369 && getDarwinState().cem1369.hasSignedUpOnce;
    var hasCreatedAccount = getDarwinState().cem1369 && getDarwinState().cem1369.hasCreatedAccount;
    var modalContainer = document.getElementsByClassName('js-modal-dialog-nl-contents')[0];
    var emailadd = getDarwinState().cem1369.hasSignedUpOnce;

    if (hasSignedUp && !hasCreatedAccount) {
      // if signed up for one newsletter as SSU, show registration modal
      // if created an account, ask to confirm

      modalContainer.classList.add('ready-reg');
      document.getElementsByClassName('newsletter-title-cem1369')[0].innerText = "";

      setTimeout(updateTitle, 500);

      // auto populate email from last sign-up (saved in local storage) and trigger keyUp validation
      document.getElementById('modal-dialog-nl-email-cem1369').value = emailadd;
      if (address !== "" || address !== null) {
        validateRegistrationAccount('');
      }

    } else if (hasSignedUp && hasCreatedAccount) {
      document.getElementsByClassName("email-var")[0].innerText = emailadd;

      if (modalContainer.classList.contains('ready-reg')) {
        modalContainer.classList.replace('ready-reg', 'reg-signup');
      } else {
        modalContainer.classList.add('reg-signup');
      }
    }

  }

  function updateTitle() {
    document.getElementsByClassName('newsletter-title-cem1369')[0].innerText = document.getElementsByClassName(
      'js-modal-dialog-nl-title')[1].innerText;
  }

"rob magazine"].indexOf(text)>=0)return classLabel+franchiseColour}else if(source==="storyBasicLabel")if(["new","breaking","breaking news","live discussion","live now","developing"].indexOf(text)>=0)return classLabel+breakingColour;else if(["earlier","updated","upcoming"].indexOf(text)>=0)return classLabel+updateColour;else if(["exclusive","investigation","in depth","explainer"].indexOf(text)>=0)return classLabel+franchiseColour;else if(["opinion"].indexOf(text)>=0)return classLabel+opinionColour;

"";if((article.label&&article.label.basic&&article.label.basic.text==="opinion"||article.subtype==="column")&&article.credits&&article.credits.by.length>=1)template=["\x3cspan class\x3d'c-card__meta-authors'\x3e","  \x3cspan class\x3d'c-card__meta-author u-no-wrap'\x3e",article.credits.by[0].name,"\x3c/span\x3e","\x3c/span\x3e"].join("");return template}function displayImage(image){var template="";if(image.path)template=["\x3cdiv class\x3d'c-image-container c-image-container--lazy' style\x3d'padding-bottom: 66.66666666666666%'\x3e",

label.colour,"'/\x3e",label.text,"\x3c/span\x3e","\x3c/span\x3e"].join("");return template}function displayDateTag(article){var isSectionPageContext=["story","video"].indexOf(window.tgam.meta.pagetype)===-1;var storyPublishedDate=storyPublishedDateTag(article);if(!storyPublishedDate||!window.Intl||!window.Intl.DateTimeFormat)return"";var storyDate=storyTimeDisplayTag({pubdate:storyPublishedDate,update:article.last_updated_date,isSectionPageContext:isSectionPageContext});var publishedDate=storyDate.published;

"");return["\x3ctime class\x3d'c-card__meta-time u-no-wrap ",publishedClassNames,"'\x3e",publishedPreface,publishedDate,"\x3c/time\x3e"," ","\x3ctime class\x3d'c-card__meta-time u-no-wrap ",updatedClassNames,"'\x3e",updatedPreface,isSectionPageContext?"":updatedDate,"\x3c/time\x3e"].join("")}function storyPublishedDateTag(story){return story.display_date||story.publish_date}function storyTimeDisplayTag(params){if(params.isSectionPageContext==null)params.isSectionPageContext=false;var pubDate=new Date(params.pubdate);
var upDate=new Date(params.update);var now=new Date;var oneYear=31536E3;var oneDay=86400;var threeDays=259200;var tenMinutes=600;var unixNow=Math.round(now.getTime()/1E3);var unixPubdate=Math.round(pubDate.getTime()/1E3);var unixUpdate=Math.round(upDate.getTime()/1E3);var timeSincePublication=unixNow-unixPubdate;var timeSinceUpdate=unixNow-unixUpdate;var pattern={year:"numeric",month:"long",day:"numeric",timeZone:"America/Toronto"};if(params.format)pattern=params.format;else if(params.isSectionPageContext&&
timeSincePublication<oneYear)pattern={month:"long",day:"numeric",timeZone:"America/Toronto"};var formatter=new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-US",pattern);var formattedPubDate=formatter.format(pubDate);var formattedUpDate=formatter.format(upDate);var publishedAndUpdatedAreTheSameDay=formattedPubDate===formattedUpDate;var publishedLessThanThreeDaysAgo=timeSincePublication<threeDays;var updatedWasLessThanThreeDaysAgo=timeSinceUpdate<threeDays;var storyWasUnderEmbargoOrSimilar=unixPubdate>unixUpdate;var storyHasBeenUpdatedMoreThanTenMinutesSincePublication=
!storyWasUnderEmbargoOrSimilar&&unixUpdate-unixPubdate>tenMinutes;var showPublishedDateAsMoment=publishedLessThanThreeDaysAgo;var showUpdatedDateAsMoment=!params.isSectionPageContext&&storyHasBeenUpdatedMoreThanTenMinutesSincePublication&&updatedWasLessThanThreeDaysAgo;var field="published";var moment=null;if(storyHasBeenUpdatedMoreThanTenMinutesSincePublication)if(publishedAndUpdatedAreTheSameDay&&timeSinceUpdate>=oneDay)field="published";else{field="both";if(params.isSectionPageContext==false)showPublishedDateAsMoment=
